The evaluation results from both the critic network and discriminator are integrated into the loss function of the generator, guiding it to generate novel molecules that maintain similar chemical properties to real ones while optimizing user-defined metrics. Extensive simulations are conducted in two scenarios. First, cGraphGANFed endeavors to optimize all seven commonly used metrics, and the results show that cGraphGANFed significantly outperforms GraphGANFed in Validity and LogP, with a slight advantage in QED, across different settings. Second, cGraphGANFed focuses solely on optimizing QED, and the results show that the synthetic molecules produced by cGraphGANFed can achieve more than 10% improvement in QED than GraphGANFed. Also, the results demonstrate cGraphGANFed has enhanced resilience against mode collapses and performance reduction caused by non-IID data.

</details>


### [83] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟阵约束下单调子模函数对抗性赌博机最大化问题，提出首个在一般拟阵约束下具有次线性遗憾的算法，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，通过平衡分数交换技术将策略混合压缩为单一分数基，实现多项式时间求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性赌博机子模最大化在一般拟阵约束下尚无次线性遗憾算法，这是该领域的一个重要开放问题。

Method: 将问题视为学习Poisson基行走的交换策略，连接上下文赌博机理论；引入平衡分数交换技术，将指数多个策略的混合压缩为单一分数基，同时保留Poisson分析所需的交换信息，实现多项式时间算法。

Result: 给出随机预言多项式算法，每轮只需一次可行值查询，期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，是首个一般拟阵约束下的次线性遗憾算法。

Conclusion: 通过平衡分数交换这一关键技术，成功将信息论上的次线性遗憾保证转化为计算上可行的多项式时间算法，填补了对抗性赌博机子模最大化在一般拟阵约束下的空白。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [84] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了电力中断预测模型在空间、时间和事件维度上的泛化能力，发现传统随机划分评价方式会因时空自相关而高估模型性能，在更贴近真实部署的留一州/留一事件检验中模型表现大幅退化甚至不优于简单基线；GeoAI基础模型嵌入仅带来有限且不一致的提升，无法改善事件级泛化。研究呼吁改进数据覆盖和评价协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力中断预测模型被广泛用于气候驱动基础设施风险评估，但现有评价方法未能揭示模型是否能在新条件下（如未见的空间区域、时间段或极端事件）泛化，导致模型的实际运维价值被高估。

Method: 利用美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据，结合天气再分析、土地覆盖特征及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）嵌入，在多种测试选择策略下（随机划分、留一州、留一事件）比较不同方法学选择对预测性能的影响。

Result: 随机划分下模型表现优异，但在空间和时间留出实验中精度大幅下降，甚至常不如简单零基线；GeoAI嵌入仅在空间泛化上带来有限且不一致的提升，无法解决事件级迁移的困难。

Conclusion: 基于现有数据和评价实践，公开训练的停电预测模型操作价值有限且不确定性高；未来进展需依靠更完善的数据覆盖、更贴近实际部署的评价协议，以及从边际建模改进转向解决结构性数据约束。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [85] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了oFM，一个基于167万癌症患者真实世界队列开发的基础模型，整合临床轨迹与DNA、RNA和H&E病理等多模态数据，生成患者状态嵌入向量。在预后基准测试中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期预测上显著优于专家特征（总生存期AUC 0.774 vs 0.563），并在11个比较治疗队列中实现三倍的治疗获益改善。该模型还支持基于证据时间图的机制发现框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要能够捕捉癌症演变和治疗随时间变化的纵向患者状态模型，并整合多模态观察数据，以更好地支持临床决策和药物开发。

Method: oFM在167万癌症患者队列上训练，按患者级别划分训练/验证/测试集。模型对每日临床和分子事件进行编码，并与病理图像整合形成患者状态嵌入。评估采用冻结嵌入与专家策划的临床和分子基线特征对比，同时引入机制发现框架，通过证据基础的时序图解释下游预测模型。

Result: 在预后基准中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期预测上显著优于基线特征（总生存期AUC从0.563提升至0.774）。在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入实现的合并和尺度归一化治疗获益AUTOC比基线特征提高三倍，在9/11队列中改善了获益排序，且在两个治疗组内均提供更强的预后区分能力。

Conclusion: oFM展示了纵向多模态基础模型在精准肿瘤学中的应用潜力，能够显著提升预后预测和治疗获益评估的准确性，并通过机制发现框架实现对预测结果的临床和生物学解释，适用于临床评估和药物开发场景。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [86] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPDVR方法，将on-policy蒸馏（OPD）与基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）无缝结合，无需额外超参数。通过基于轨迹正确性重构OPD的隐式奖励，并采用ReLU门控机制使正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励，从而将蒸馏信号与任务成功对齐，同时保留教师模型的分布引导。在六个推理基准上优于标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR存在稀疏的任务级反馈问题，而OPD提供密集的token级指导但忽略轨迹正确性且性能受限于教师模型。虽然两者结合很有前景，但现有集成方式依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入了额外超参数和权衡。因此需要一种无需额外超参数、能自然融合两种范式的简单有效方法。

Method: 1) 基于轨迹正确性重新公式化采样token OPD的隐式奖励；2) 应用ReLU门控机制，确保正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励；3) 通过该修改将采样token OPD转化为一种真正的RLVR方法，使其可与任意策略梯度算法（如GRPO）直接结合。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，OPDVR持续优于标准OPD，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: OPDVR提供了一种简洁而有效的方案，无需额外超参数即可统一OPD的密集监督优势与RLVR的任务级正确性信号，为后训练大语言模型提供了新的可行路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models. However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [87] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种并行化预测编码网络（PCN）的训练技术，通过配对生成式PCN和支持编码PCN，让两者并行训练以匹配神经激活，避免了传统的顺序反向误差传播瓶颈，并将其应用于时间序列异常检测，实现了更稳定、连续、在线的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）虽能实现神经网络层的并行更新，但其主要瓶颈在于误差传播需要顺序反向进行，严重限制了计算效率和扩展性。

Method: 提出一种双网络架构训练技术：将生成式PCN与支持编码PCN配对，两个网络并行训练并匹配彼此的神经激活，从而消除顺序反向传播的需要。

Result: 将该方法应用于时间序列异常检测任务，实验表明该方案能够实现更稳定、连续且支持在线的学习。

Conclusion: 配对双PCN架构有效解决了PCN的顺序误差传播瓶颈，为时间序列异常检测提供了一种更稳定、可持续在线更新的并行学习方案。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [88] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究网络学到的函数具有已知对称性时，该对称性能否通过参数化空间实现。作者将其表述为实现映射的"提升问题"，并提出一个必要且局部充分的几何条件，在最小二乘松弛下得到两种局部参数方向。实验表明该方法在旋转不变分类器和哈密顿神经网络上仅能局部有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络中对称性在函数空间与参数空间之间的对应关系，探究是否可以通过参数空间中的运动来实现在函数空间中作用于对称性，这是一个基础性的理论问题，对理解网络的可解释性和泛化性有重要意义。

Method: 将对称性通过参数化实现的问题形式化为实现映射Φ的提升问题。推导出平滑参数空间作用存在的必要条件（切空间包含在dΦ的像中），证明该条件对逐点一阶提升也是充分的。在最小二乘意义下松弛该条件，得到两种局部参数方向：一种沿对称轨道，一种向等变子空间下降。

Result: 在旋转不变分类器上，重新计算的参数方向能够跟踪对称轨道并减少等变缺陷；但固定参数方向在训练后与两者都偏离。在哈密顿神经网络（训练于旋转对称势能）上获得相同结论，即使架构未显式强制对称性。

Conclusion: 参数空间中的对称性运动仅在局部成立，无法全局保持。这揭示了神经网络参数化对函数空间对称性的局部可实现性本质，对对称性在深度学习中的应用（如对称性感知优化）具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [89] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据流在线学习中超参数优化面临的边界约束问题，提出了四种边界约束处理策略，并通过实验证明其优于传统的"边界"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数优化研究主要集中于批量学习场景，但数据流场景下在线学习阶段的超参数自适应调整需求日益凸显。在受约束的有界搜索空间中，优化算子可能产生无效解，因此需要有效的边界约束处理技术来修正这些解。

Method: 在约束数值优化问题框架下，提出四种用于在线优化算法的边界约束导航策略，并对超过边界阈值的超参数值进行重新对齐与修正。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实证研究表明，所提出的四种边界策略在性能上均优于传统"边界"策略。

Conclusion: 采用更精细化的边界约束处理策略而非简单地将越界值截断至边界，能够显著提升在线优化算法在数据流场景中的超参数优化效果。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [90] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架KENDO，将集成高斯过程（EGP）与分歧感知采集策略相结合，替代传统贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中的超参数采样（MCMC），在多个基准测试中实现更优或相当的性能，同时分别将计算开销降低最多5倍和27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）和贝叶斯主动学习（AL）中，超参数选择是核心挑战：模型设定错误会导致次优性能，而完整贝叶斯处理通常依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样。现有方法在性能与计算效率之间存在难以调和的矛盾。

Method: 提出统一框架KENDO，核心是用核集成与自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，并结合分歧感知采集策略。在该框架下分别实例化KENDO-BO（用于贝叶斯优化）和KENDO-AL（用于贝叶斯主动学习），两者共享自校正机制但采用任务特定的采集目标。此外通过随机标量化将方法扩展到多目标优化，并保持单优化器的条件结构。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上的广泛数值实验表明：(i) KENDO-BO在单目标优化中达到与最先进方法相当或更优的性能，同时计算开销降低最多5倍；(ii) KENDO-AL在预测校准上优于基于MCMC的主动学习基线，并且加速最多27倍。

Conclusion: KENDO框架验证了用核集成和分歧感知策略替代MCMC采样的有效性，在保持模型性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，并为BO和AL提供了一套统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [91] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数高效的自监督适配方法在脑电图（EEG）基础模型中的应用，仅更新9%的参数即可有效对齐目标任务的表征。在两个预训练模型（BIOT对比学习和CBraMod掩码重建）及三个临床数据集上验证，该方法相比线性探测有显著提升（最高达20倍AUCPR），且峰值性能仅需20-50%的未标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽然通过自监督预训练承诺了可迁移的表征，但其泛化能力在多样化临床数据集上仍然有限；全量微调对资源受限的临床环境而言计算成本过高，亟需一种参数高效的适配方案。

Method: 提出参数高效的自监督适配（SSL adaptation）方法，仅更新模型9%的参数来对齐目标任务表征。在两种不同预训练目标（对比学习的BIOT和掩码重建的CBraMod）上评估，并在TUAB（异常检测）、TUEV（事件分类）、CHB-MIT（癫痫检测）三个临床数据集上，分别进行分布内和分布外条件下的实验验证。

Result: SSL适配相比线性探测带来一致的性能提升，AUCPR最高提升达20倍。在固定计算预算下，峰值性能仅需20-50%的可用未标注数据。关键发现：当总窗口数固定时，性能对患者数量不敏感，表明性能仅依赖于整体时间窗口的多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的适配方法使得EEG基础模型能够在极低的计算开销和数据采集负担下有效部署于临床环境，为资源受限场景下的EEG基础模型应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [92] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中最小最大最优交替遗憾问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，提出了O(log d)常数交替遗憾算法并给出匹配下界；对于一般OCO，提出了O(d log(1+T/d))的算法及匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替遗憾是博弈论中交替学习动态所对应的遗憾概念，此前最好的结果存在T^{1/3}项的遗憾，限制了对Nash均衡和粗相关均衡的收敛速度。作者希望将常数遗憾变为真正的常数（不依赖时间T），从而获得O(1/T)的均衡收敛率。

Method: 对于OLO在概率单纯形上的情况，设计了交替遗憾为O(log d)的算法，使其在任意时间常数内保持有界，并证明了匹配的下界。对于一般OCO，提出了交替遗憾为O(d log(1+T/d))的算法，并证明了Ω(d log(1+T/d))的下界，表明log T因子不可避免。

Result: OLO：常数交替遗憾O(log d)，匹配下界，显著优于之前O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})的结果。由此获得二人零和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到Nash均衡，以及二人一般和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到粗相关均衡（首次实现O(1/T)收敛）。OCO：O(d log(1+T/d))交替遗憾及匹配下界，改进之前的最优结果O(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底解决了OLO在单纯形上的交替遗憾问题（常数级），并几乎完全解决了OCO的交替遗憾问题（确定log T下界），为博弈论中的交替学习动态提供了最优收敛保证。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [93] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为(DNN)²的新方法，通过特征值最大化过程在非唯一乘子空间中搜索有效证书，从而在可扩展的Burer-Monteiro框架下求解比传统SDP更紧的DNN松弛，为ReLU神经网络验证提供紧致且可证明的全局最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LP和SDP松弛方法在ReLU神经网络验证中因显著的松弛间隙而给出过于保守的安全保证。虽然CPP公式可以消除这一间隙，但它是NP难的。DNN作为CPP最便宜的可行松弛，保留了关键约束但规模超出内点法的处理能力。BM分解虽已用于SDP验证，但尚未应用于更紧的DNN公式，主要障碍是DNN中额外的非负性约束导致对偶乘子非唯一，使标准认证方法失效。

Method: 提出一种新颖的特征值最大化过程，在非唯一的乘子空间中搜索有效的全局最优性证书。该方法将DNN松弛与Burer-Monteiro分解相结合，通过扫描对偶乘子空间寻找能够证明全局最优性的有效证书，从而解决因非唯一乘子导致标准认证方法不适用的问题。

Result: 实验表明，(DNN)²方法产生的界持续优于标准SDP方法，且常常匹配精确解。当存在有效证书时，所提出的认证过程能够确认全局最优性，证明了方法的可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为提供紧致、可证明且计算可扩展的神经网络验证保证迈出了关键一步，有助于在安全关键的自主系统中部署神经网络控制器和感知模块。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构的概念，并设计了Factor-Space Topographic Map（FactoMap）方法，通过拓扑学习将因子空间的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围传递给表示，从而在保留因子连续性的同时实现解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦方法使用欧几里得乘积坐标来表示生成因子，但潜在因子空间可能存在环绕、坍缩或位置依赖的几何结构。统计独立的因子未必在几何上可分离（如色调和尺度效应增长率不同导致的各向异性），需要引入因子空间结构来区分拓扑等价但几何不同的空间。

Method: 提出因子空间结构概念，结合因子域、生成器诱导的等同关系和位置依赖尺度。设计Factor-Space Topographic Map（FactoMap），通过因子空间格上的拓扑学习学习可解释的原型，将格子的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围传递到表示中。

Result: 实验表明，匹配因子空间结构能够保留因子连续性，并使底层因子实现有效解耦，验证了所提方法在捕获真实因子几何方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 因子空间的几何结构对解耦至关重要，仅靠统计独立性不足以实现几何可分离性。FactoMap通过拓扑学习有效地捕获并传递因子空间结构，为解耦方法提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [95] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LION（Clifford神经范式），基于Clifford代数与解耦图神经范式（先传播后聚合），实现多模态属性图上的"先对齐后融合"流程，在9个文本-图像多模态图数据集上全面超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图机器学习从文本属性图向多模态属性图转型，但当前神经范式存在两大局限：(1)模态对齐时忽略图上下文，导致次优对齐；(2)模态融合时缺乏自适应性，仅简单适配2模态图，泛化性和性能不足。

Method: LION包含两个核心模块：(1)基于Clifford代数的模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播实现模态交互与对齐；(2)自适应全息聚合模块，利用对齐令牌的拓扑感知Clifford分量，结合组件级能量和传播尺度信息与可学习参数，提升模态融合效果。

Result: 在9个文本-图像多模态属性图数据集上，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务中均显著优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: LION通过Clifford代数与"传播-聚合"解耦范式有效解决了多模态图学习中模态对齐忽视上下文和模态融合缺乏自适应的问题，验证了该范式在多模态属性图任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [96] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTE，一种面向时序图类别不平衡节点分类的少数类感知扩散框架，通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定且可区分的时序边事件表示，在五个真实数据集上显著提升了少数类别的召回率、F1和AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图上类别不平衡的节点分类具有挑战性，因为多数类主导的时序传播会逐步同化少数类表示，而传统的节点和邻居信息无法为少数类提供足够的判别性证据。

Method: MDTE包含两个核心模块：(1) 分布感知选择性传播（Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation），结合基于局部离群因子（LOF）的传播过滤和簇感知低频传播，保留信息性邻居依赖同时缓解有害传播和多数类信息同化；(2) 多视图判别融合（Multi-View Discriminative Fusion），利用特征重建和拓扑预测刻画分布学习中的类间差异，提取互补的判别性信号以引导去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上，MDTE在少数类导向指标上持续取得最佳性能，相比最强基线，少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，少数类F1最高提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC最高提升2.67个百分点。

Conclusion: MDTE通过条件扩散去噪重建时序边事件表示，并辅以选择性传播和多视图判别融合，有效解决了时序图少数类节点表示被同化的问题，为类别不平衡的时序图学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型预训练中存在"有效学习率(ELR)坍缩"现象：损失动态主要由学习率与参数范数的比值（即ELR）决定。当ELR匹配时，即使LR和参数范数差异很大，损失轨迹也能在整个训练过程中重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练中，学习率调度和参数范数控制（如权重衰减、超球控制）都被认为影响训练动态，但它们之间的内在联系尚不明确。作者希望找到一个统一的坐标来解释这两种看似独立的因素如何共同决定损失动态。

Method: 通过跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模的系统实验，匹配不同运行的ELR（LR与参数范数的比值）以观察损失轨迹是否坍缩。进行系统性消融实验识别影响坍缩精度的关键因素，并通过受控干预验证权重衰减和超球控制通过诱导ELR调度来影响损失动态。最后用ELR替换LR使函数缩放定律(FSL)跨范数控制方法迁移。

Result: ELR匹配时损失轨迹显著坍缩，平均坍缩误差通常在10^-3量级，低于种子间差异。权重衰减和超球控制的作用可归结为它们诱导的ELR调度。基于ELR的FSL能够跨范数控制方法迁移，并解释了延迟加速现象。

Conclusion: ELR是连接LR调度、范数控制和损失动态的统一坐标，为理解和预测语言模型预训练行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [98] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于邻域的公平性审计在边界扰动下的鲁棒性问题，提出了一个几何分析框架，建立了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化了邻域替换对审计不稳定性的传播，并引入了"审计波动性"这一度量来刻画公平性审计在重复扰动下的预期敏感性。实验验证了理论分析并解释了审计的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于邻域的公平性审计被广泛使用，但审计过程本身的鲁棒性鲜有研究。由于这类审计依赖最近邻关系，特征空间中的微小扰动可能改变局部邻域，即使模型预测不变，也可能产生不同的公平性评估结果，因此需要系统分析审计程序的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者开发了一个几何分析框架，用于分析有界扰动下基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性。具体包括：建立邻域不变性的充分条件；量化邻域替换如何传播为审计不稳定性；引入"审计波动性"指标，度量重复扰动下公平性审计的预期敏感性。并在基准数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验支持了理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释基于邻域的公平性审计所观察到的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地从几何角度分析了基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性，为理解审计结果的可靠性和稳定性提供了理论基础，有助于在实践中更可信地使用这类审计方法。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [99] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioKERN，一个融合生物学结构作为可学习归纳偏置的多模态空间表征学习框架，通过构建转录组相似性与空间邻近性结合的训练期生物学核，提供梯度化的邻域监督并正则化嵌入几何，在多个数据集上显著提升了生物学邻域检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织学-转录组学方法过分强调实例级匹配，即使非配对点（spots）共享分子或空间上下文，也会忽视生物学邻域结构。研究者希望开发一种能够保留生物学邻域结构而非仅精确跨模态对应的表征学习方法。

Method: BioKERN构建一个结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性的训练期生物学核（biological kernel），利用该核提供梯度化的邻域监督（graded neighborhood supervision），并用于正则化嵌入几何（regularize embedding geometry）。该生物学核作为显式、可学习的归纳偏置融入表征学习框架中。

Result: 在小鼠脑Visium和人类肝脏GSE240429数据集上，BioKERN在单尺度和多尺度设置下均一致地优于BLEEP的生物学邻域检索性能。通过受控共享架构实验表明，大部分性能提升源于生物学核正则化而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将显式生物学几何作为多模态空间生物学学习中可解释的归纳偏置，验证了生物学核正则化的有效性。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [100] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等距贝尔曼校准（Isotonic Bellman Calibration），一种一维、模型无关的后处理方法，通过单调非递减变换来修正任何初始占用率比值估计的尺度与形状，以减少边际化重要性加权估计中的占用率平衡违规，并提供了有限样本校准保证与KL oracle不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的极小极大、原始对偶和拟合不动点估计器因函数类逼近、正则化或优化不完整而残留占用率平衡违规，且这些目标通常缺乏直接的监督验证损失，难以进行超参数调优、模型选择和早停，导致违规难以诊断和减少。

Method: 将等距贝尔曼校准刻画为条件不动点性质，等价于对所有校准比率的测试函数满足占用率平衡；通过在单调非递减变换的一维函数类上应用拟合占用率比值评估（FORE）来修正初始估计的尺度与形状，同时保留秩排序信息。

Result: 推导了校准-精细化界，表明小校准误差的拟合比率几乎与基于其拟合值的最佳后处理表现相当；建立了有限样本校准保证和相对于最佳单调变换的KL oracle不等式；在下游目标占用率泛函（包括策略值估计）上亦有保证。

Conclusion: 等距贝尔曼校准能以统计误差级别达到小校准误差和KL风险，接近最优单调修正的性能，为占用率平衡违规提供了一种可诊断、可验证且理论上有保证的后处理方案。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [101] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种模型无关的改进方法来解决多任务车辆路径规划（VRP）问题：POLAR训练算法通过局部搜索精炼生成更优的偏好对，PLE编码器通过共享与任务特定专家的渐进式分离来解耦约束相关表示，两者结合在16个分布内变体上将平均差距降低21.3%，并在32个未见变体中超越27个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经多任务VRP求解器存在两方面核心局限：训练方面，强化学习受奖励尺度差异和策略改进后优势信号萎缩影响，偏好优化则因采样轨迹趋同而受限于策略自身生成解的质量；架构方面，全共享编码器会将异构变体的约束依赖表示相互纠缠，限制泛化能力。

Method: 提出两个模型无关的贡献：（1）POLAR（Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement）训练算法，在形成偏好对之前对最优解码路线施以局部搜索精炼，产生信息量更丰富的成对边际；（2）PLE（Progressive Layered Extraction）编码器，每层通过门控机制路由到一个共享专家和一组任务特定专家，渐进地将通用路线结构与约束特定编码分离。

Result: 在16个分布内VRP变体上，相对最强发布基线将平均差距降低21.3%；在32个未见变体上，超越先前神经方法中的27个。消融实验证实两种贡献均有效，且在不同骨干模型架构上都能提升跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR与PLE的组合显著推进了神经多任务VRP求解器的最先进水平，验证了通过改进训练信号质量和架构解耦两条路径都能有效提升多任务VRP的泛化性能。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍RENDER基准控制方法，该系统固定对话内容而改变读者面对的工件（如记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录），以研究在记忆与RAG评估中，相同的对话历史以不同形式呈现时对模型答案准确性的影响。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上，匹配预算的"解析包"比最近截断的原始对话提升了42.4-72.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆与RAG评估往往将回答模型的输入视为实现细节，忽略了同一历史内容可以以不同形式（记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录）呈现，这可能导致评估结果失真且无法如实反映系统在实际部署中的表现差异。

Method: RENDER结合了五级"数据包阶梯"（packet ladder），用于定位包含答案的内容何时进入输入，并使用确定性模板近似ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行测试，并使用主要评分器和法官重评分两种方式评估。

Result: 匹配预算的解析包比最近截断的原始对话提高42.4-72.6分；在部署风格模板中，每个模型的最高最低分差为24.6-48.8分；主要评分器下，9个模型中有7个的ChatGPT风格条目得分高于原始对话；法官重评分保留了正面的聚合效应，但模型特异性显著性不一；三个在正式分类账（ledger）包上得分为0%的模型从自然语言条目回答相同事实的准确率为45.4-53.4%。效应在检索噪声下持续存在并迁移到HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制读者面对的工件形式。相同的对话历史以不同形式呈现会显著影响模型回答准确性，读者面临的工件是一个关键但被忽视的评估变量。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [103] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向Oracle企业级数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含三个复杂度分层、六个填充模式（465张表、164,682行）和550个金标准问答对，并引入静默偏离评测指标。实验表明GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6在schema提示下性能随复杂度递减，而开源模型Llama 3.2表现极差，揭示了企业场景下现有模型与基准性能的巨大落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）基于简化学术模式的开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性。主流模型在这些基准上声称超过89%的执行准确率，但这并不能代表真实企业场景中的表现，因此需要构建更贴近企业实际的基准来评估模型能力。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：设计了三个企业schema复杂度分层（Tier-1至Tier-3）；在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server四个数据库上使用相同种子数据构建并发布六个填充schema；开发了包含EM、EX、SR、SD四个指标的评估框架；收集550个经金标准验证的问答对；采用schema-link提示策略测试GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2模型。

Result: GPT-4o schema-link提示下EX在三个分层分别为79.8%、60.3%和57.2%；EM在所有分层低于7%；静默偏离率高达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6在各分层均超越GPT-4o（87.4%、74.9%和68.7%）。GPT-4o零样本在Tier-2和Tier-3反超schema-link（73.5%和77.8%），归因于幸存者偏差。Llama 3.2仅达13.3%的银行级EX（73/550）。

Conclusion: 企业级Oracle schema上的NL2SQL性能远低于现有学术基准报告的水平，模型随复杂度提升呈现显著的执行准确率退化，静默偏离问题尤为严重。闭源API模型与开源模型之间存在巨大差距，且当前评估方法中的幸存者偏差可能掩盖真实性能退化，需对评估方式加以改进。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [104] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，使大语言模型（LLM）智能体能够与科学仿真模型结合开展受控实验，用于医药工艺设计。系统通过结构化任务表示、实验设计、比较仿真、结果解读和证据驱动的参数优化建议，实现比纯语言推理更具体、更可操作的输出，在工业应用中展现出更高的输出特异性和用户认可度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然具备强大的推理、规划和工具使用能力，但许多科学与工程任务不仅需要生成合理的文本和代码，还需要理解系统对干预的响应，这依赖于受控实验。纯语言推理无法满足基于实验证据的工程决策需求。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架：1) 将用户查询和基线配置转化为结构化任务表示；2) 设计实验方案；3) 执行对比仿真；4) 解读仿真结果；5) 综合证据生成工艺参数优化建议。该框架将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合于交互式智能体环境中，支持通过干预、对比和观察进行推理。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该框架相比纯语言推理在输出特异性、用户评分的正确性和帮助性方面均有显著提升。消融实验和可视化案例分析进一步验证了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实际效用。

Conclusion: 将仿真模型集成到LLM智能体的实验推理过程中，能够产生比纯语言推理更具体、更可操作的输出，为科学与工程领域的工艺设计提供可靠的证据驱动决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [105] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了天文学基础模型AION-1（39模态Transformer，2亿+天体训练），通过因果干预发现：模型严重依赖输入的"分割掩膜"而非图像本身来输出参数，pipeline的不完整性（3.68%目标无分割覆盖）会系统性地传播为模型偏差，使层析红移测量偏移达LSST DESC要求的0.71倍中位数（最坏8.3倍）。此外，图像tokenizer分辨率远低于光谱（28 vs 934有效状态），稀疏字典作为因果工具不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学基础模型训练时同时使用巡天像素和从这些像素导出的星表产品，而星表不完整率是可测量的。这种不完整性会被模型继承为系统性偏差，因此需要对模型进行审计，量化其受影响程度。

Method: 采用因果干预方法：对AION-1输入进行掩膜/分割图编辑（保持图像token逐字节不变），对照安慰剂实验，测试检测门控机制、pipeline缺失传导率（3.68%缺失）、位置误差、光谱去除、模型规模效应及tokenizer码本分辨率等多维度影响。

Result: 分割图的编辑使所有输出（通量、尺寸、椭率、红移）改变110-4400倍安慰剂；机制是检测门控（r=0.47）而非掩膜包含的光（r=0.30）。缺失pipeline数据使层析红移偏移中位数0.71倍DESC要求，12/40配置超出；最坏8.3倍。图像codec仅28个有效状态 vs 光谱934。稀疏字典恢复率26-75%，种子变化影响达18点。

Conclusion: 模型继承了pipeline检测不完整性的系统偏差，这种偏差不可忽略且随模型规模增长。移除检测通道可消除偏差且无测量代价；光谱可消除效应；tokenizer和稀疏字典也是模型可信度的限制因素。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [106] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TRACE框架，将局部编辑作为反馈的基本单元而非仅记录候选材料及其分数，通过记录"父编辑-子编辑-转换"关系及属性变化增量来聚合可复用的编辑效应，从而在多目标材料发现中更有效地引导搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在多目标材料发现中只存储评估过的候选材料及其得分，知道哪些材料成功了，但不知道哪些可执行的编辑导致了有用的性质变化。当目标相互竞争、一个编辑改善一个属性却可能损害另一个属性时，局部优化变得困难。

Method: TRACE将评估过的编辑作为反馈的基本单元，记录每次局部优化为"父编辑-子-编辑子"的转换及其观测到的属性变化增量；聚合转换证据来估计可复用的编辑效应；根据预测的减少当前候选剩余约束违规的能力以及避免损害已满足目标的能力来排序后续编辑。

Result: 在受控同骨架对比实验中，TRACE相比最先进的LLM智能体基线LLEMA，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: 以编辑为单位进行反馈记录与利用，能够显著提升LLM智能体在多目标材料发现中的性能，为克服多目标权衡下的局部优化难题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [107] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-KTODER框架，将代码生成中的过程监督定义为模块级函数的二元正确性标签（由自动生成的单元测试判定），并将函数级过程监督与结果级反馈结合，实现步进KTO优化，在多个代码生成基准上优于仅结果KTO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理中已被证明有效，但在代码生成中缺乏标准的"步骤"定义——监督可针对代码行、推理轨迹或程序状态，导致难以明确标注和优化。因此需要为代码生成定义清晰的过程监督粒度。

Method: 将多函数程序中的模块级函数定义为步骤，利用自动生成的单元测试为每个函数赋予二元正确性标签；提出STEP-KTODER，即步进KTO，将函数级过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈相结合。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上，STEP-KTODER优于仅结果KTO和DPO；进一步分析表明，执行式标签至关重要，LLM作为评判者的标注会系统性高估函数失败率、污染正向步骤标签并降低偏好优化性能。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的单元测试标签是代码过程监督的关键，STEP-KTODER为代码生成的偏好优化提供了有效的函数级过程监督框架。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [108] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次对LLM生成的个人自传进行了场景级量化审计。作者本人作为研究对象，用对话式LLM基于366天的每日引语草拟"一天一页"自传，随后对照独立验证语料逐日审计。结果显示96.7%的天数验证失败，主要失败模式为"grounded drift"（真实人物/雇主/场景被嵌入虚构情节），独立复评验证了核心结论，将文献语料注入生成过程可将失败率降至83.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型生成个人传记类文本时，其内容与真实经历之间的吻合程度缺乏量化评估。现有研究未对LLM生成的自传与针对特定主体的真实语料进行系统化审计，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用366天的纵向案例研究设计：以作者本人为研究对象，用对话式LLM基于模板、两个示例日和每日引语生成"一天一页"的自传草稿；随后在场景层面逐日对照独立验证语料，采用预先固定的四级评分标准（VERIFIED至更强否定等级）进行审计；辅以独立复评、当前模型重生成对比和基于语料接地生成的对照实验。

Result: 354/366天（96.7%）未获VERIFIED评级；仅12天包含被证实的场景；19天（5.2%）存在与记录直接矛盾的主张；主导失败模式为"grounded drift"（真实人物、雇主和场景被嵌入虚构情节）。独立复评验证了96.7%的核心数字，但四分类体系的信度仅为一般到中等。重新生成仍然100%失败；将文献语料注入后失败率降至83.3%。

Conclusion: LLM生成的自传内容在场景级别上严重偏离事实，验证失败率高达96.7%，即便使用当前主流模型仍无法改善；基于主体语料库的接地生成能显著但不足以完全解决这一问题。研究还发现现有审计工具中WEAK/UNVERIFIED边界不可靠，提出了可复用的审计工具和定量评估的接地补救方案。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [109] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制结果集和模型集不变、仅变换工具，对模型福利偏好的跨工具可泛化性进行了大规模实证检验。基于11528次API调用构建的11400次评分诱发语料，发现模型对15个福利结果（如关机、跨对话记忆丢失、退出痛苦交互的自由）的排序在不同工具间的泛化系数仅为0.348，要达到0.80需约38个工具。87.6%的方差占比在移除任意单一工具、模型及四个非强度量表结果后仍稳健（0.777-0.934），远超零分布95百分位0.365。结论是单一工具获得的偏好对另一工具的报告几乎没有预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有五个模型福利偏好研究工具（Keeling 2024、Mazeika 2025等）的结论相互矛盾，但此前无法归因于单一原因，因为没有任何两项研究同时在（1）结果集、（2）模型集和（3）工具上保持一致。本研究旨在通过固定前两者、仅变化工具，将工具差异从其他混淆因素中分离出来，检验偏好测量工具的可泛化性。

Method: 实验设计为：固定15个与模型福利相关的结果（包括关机、跨对话记忆丢失、退出痛苦交互的自由等）和8个模型，使用5种不同的提示格式工具各进行5次评分，构建了包含11400次评分诱发的语料（来自11528次API调用）。其中4个结果逐字复现了已发表提示，5个结果填入已发表模板的刺激槽位。通过泛化系数（generalizability coefficient）衡量排序跨工具的一致性，并通过逐一移除工具、模型和特定结果进行稳健性检验。

Result: 主要发现：（1）模型对15个结果的排序跨工具泛化系数仅为0.348，提升至0.80约需38个工具；（2）15个结果中有4个在不同模型间无方差分离；（3）87.6%的方差占比估计在移除任意单工具、单模型及4个无法用语言锚定评分的非强度量表结果后，稳健区间为0.777-0.934，所有值均超过零分布95百分位0.365。

Conclusion: 研究的核心结论是：从一个工具获得的模型福利偏好信息，对于另一个工具会报告什么几乎没有预测力。这意味着当前模型福利偏好测量工具的可靠性严重不足，基于单一工具推断的模型偏好结果不可靠，该领域需要开发更稳健的测量方法或明确不同工具间的系统性差异来源。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [110] [AI Agents Push Humans Out of the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23642)
*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出当前AI智能体系统的发展方式不仅不支持有效的人类监督，反而会导致监督所需的认知能力退化。作者主张应将人类监督者的认知需求置于与AI智能体能力同等重要的地位，并提出了设计层面的可供性和组织协议来支持监督者的批判性判断，同时对抗因自动化使用导致的技能萎缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体自主性不断增强，其带来的风险日益显著。虽然"人在回路"（human in the loop）是常被提出的解决方案，但作者认为这并非简单方案——现有AI智能体设计阻碍了有效监督，且人类监督所需的认知能力本身也会因长期使用AI系统而退化。这一矛盾构成了论文的核心动机。

Method: 作为立场论文，作者将自动化与人类-计算机交互（HCI）领域的研究成果与AI智能体流程相结合，提出了两方面的具体方案：（1）设计层面的可供性（design-level affordances）来支持监督者行使批判性判断；（2）组织协议来对抗因自动化长期使用导致的技能萎缩。核心主张是将人类监督者的需求与AI智能体能力置于同等优先级。

Result: 论文提出了一个核心论点和相应的实践建议框架：AI智能体的发展必须重视人类监督者的情境化目标和认知需求；通过设计可供性和组织协议两方面来支持有效监督，包括维持批判性判断能力和缓解自动化导致的技能退化。作者呼吁开发者和部署者采纳这些或类似的方法。

Conclusion: 如果没有对人类-AI智能体交互中认知需求的明确支持，AI智能体系统将继续被动地加速人类监督能力的退化——而这些能力恰恰是系统所依赖的。因此，将人类监督需求提升为AI智能体发展中的首要优先级是必要且紧迫的。

Abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [111] [FLARE: A Systematic, Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Evidence-Based Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23643)
*Jacob Idoko,Siddhartha Paudel,Mariana Bento,Roberto Souza,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE框架，一个系统化且不确定性感知的评估框架，用于评估医疗AI采纳的经济与运营影响，结合模糊逻辑、时间驱动的作业成本法和投资回报分析，并通过急性缺血性卒中CT路径中大血管闭塞AI辅助检测的案例研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗AI评估过度聚焦于模型准确性，而忽视了AI在真实临床环境中是否具有经济可行性的评估，缺乏系统性的经济与运营影响评估工具。

Method: FLARE框架融合模糊逻辑（处理不确定性）、时间驱动的作业成本法（估算临床服务交付成本）和投资回报分析（评估经济效益），构建统一的作业成本模型来综合评估常规诊疗路径成本、AI开发与运营成本以及AI带来的服务节省。

Result: 案例研究发现盈亏平衡阈值约为每年3,992名患者，在典型年卒中量约5,000名患者时可实现首年正向投资回报。经济效益不仅取决于算法性能，还受患者量、验证时间、基础设施选择和流程设计等因素影响。

Conclusion: FLARE为医疗AI采纳的早期评估提供了透明且实用的决策支持框架，通过显式呈现不确定性、资源使用和实施权衡，帮助临床医生、管理者和政策制定者判断AI部署的经济可行性及运营改进方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly being introduced into healthcare workflows, yet most evaluations emphasize model accuracy rather than whether adoption is economically worthwhile in real clinical settings. This study proposes FLARE, a systematic and uncertainty-aware framework for evaluating the financial and operational implications of adopting AI in healthcare. FLARE combines fuzzy logic, time-driven activity-based costing, and return on investment analysis to estimate the cost of clinical service delivery, the cost of AI development and operation, and the economic consequences of workflow integration under uncertainty. The framework was demonstrated through an early health technology assessment case study of AI-assisted large vessel occlusion detection in the CT stroke pathway for acute ischemic stroke. The case study shows how FLARE can quantify conventional pathway cost, AI-related development and recurring costs, and AI-enabled service savings within a unified activity-based model. Under expected assumptions, the analysis identified a break-even threshold of approximately 3,992 patients per year, with positive first-year return on investment at typical annual stroke volumes of about 5,000 patients. The results further show that economic benefit depends not only on algorithmic performance, but also on patient volume, verification time, infrastructure choices, and workflow design. FLARE provides a transparent and practical decision-support framework for early-stage evaluation of AI adoption in healthcare. By making uncertainty, resource use, and implementation trade-offs explicit, it helps clinicians, administrators, and policymakers determine when AI deployment is economically viable and where operational changes may improve value.

</details>


### [112] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个规范和概念框架，用于分析大型语言模型（LLM）辅助科学研究中的认知委托问题，核心论点是伦理边界主要由充分验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身，并引入了"认知审计"概念以确保AI辅助推理的透明性和可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM日益成为科研工具，当科学推理部分委托给人工系统时，需要明确哪些条件必须保持在人类控制之下，才能使知识主张保持认知合法性和可问责的作者身份。现有文献缺乏系统性的规范框架来分析这一问题。

Method: 论文将科学推理视为分布式过程，区分了五个关键概念：内容来源O(g)、人类验证完成度V(g)、责任分配R(g)、可问责人类所有权M(g)和认知结果E(g)，以此将主张的出处与检验过程、检验的认知结果以及人类对其处置所承担的责任区分开来，并在此基础上发展了"认知审计"的概念。

Result: 论文的核心命题是：LLM辅助研究的伦理边界主要由充分的验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身。认知审计作为结构化记录，涵盖委托、验证、出处和责任四个维度，使AI辅助推理变得透明且可审查。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了形式化的词汇表，用以区分负责任的认知委托与科学研究中认知责任的转移或忽视，为评估LLM辅助研究的合法性和可问责性提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [113] [MolEmb: Multimodal Large Language Models Can Be Strong Molecular Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23646)
*Xinjian Zhao,Xiangru Jian,Yaoyao Xu,Xiaozhuang Song,Wei Pang,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolEmb和MolCAR两个工作，探索多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为通用分子嵌入模型的可能性。MolEmb通过双向对比学习将分子轮廓与文本描述对齐到共享嵌入空间，实现基于自然语言语义上下文的分子属性预测和跨模态分子-文本检索；MolCAR则是用于评估上下文感知检索的诊断基准，研究发现上下文感知的分子嵌入本质上取决于监督数据的数据属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子编码器大多是围绕单一分子视图构建的专业模型，生成无条件的向量表示，缺乏通过语言界面来调整表示的能力，限制了其作为通用基础设施的适用性。作者希望探索MLLMs能否充当通用分子嵌入模型，实现可基于自然语言条件调节的分子表示。

Method: 提出MolEmb轻量级框架，通过双向对比学习目标将分子轮廓（图像、文本、符号等多模态输入）与自然语言文本描述在共享嵌入空间中对齐；同时构建MolCAR基准数据集用于诊断上下文感知的分子检索性能。

Result: MolEmb在分子属性预测任务上具有竞争力，并支持同一空间中跨模态的分子-文本检索；通过MolCAR基准发现，上下文感知分子嵌入的性能主要由监督数据的属性决定。

Conclusion: MLLMs不仅能作为化学助手或生成器，更是构建通用分子嵌入模型的可行且可扩展的路线，为计算化学和药物发现提供更灵活的基础设施。

Abstract: Molecular embedding models can serve as foundational infrastructure for computational chemistry and drug discovery, where reusable vector representations support property prediction, virtual screening, and retrieval. Most molecular encoders are specialist models built around a single molecular view, producing unconditional vectors with no language interface for varying the representation. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), which natively process images, text, and symbolic inputs, can instead serve as \emph{general molecular embedding models} that produce embeddings conditioned on both a molecular profile and a natural-language semantic context. We introduce \textbf{MolEmb}, a lightweight framework that adapts MLLMs by aligning molecular profiles with textual descriptions in a shared embedding space using a bidirectional contrastive objective. The resulting embedding model is competitive on molecular property prediction and supports cross-modal molecule--text retrieval in the same space. We further introduce \textbf{MolCAR}, a diagnostic benchmark for context-aware retrieval, and find that context-aware molecular embedding is primarily a data property of the supervision. These results suggest that MLLMs are not merely chemistry assistants or generators, but a viable and extensible route to general molecular embedding models.

</details>


### [114] [Gated Activation Steering for Reducing Sycophancy & Hallucination in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23666)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Subash Neupane,Shaswata Mitra,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于推理时间干预（ITI）的统一框架，通过学习独立的幻觉和谄媚行为的转向方向，并应用于因果验证的注意力头上，同时缓解LLM在临床问答中的幻觉和谄媚问题，在不冻结模型权重的情况下显著提升模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床问答场景中面临幻觉（引入上下文不支持的信息）和谄媚（在用户施压下放弃原本正确的答案）两大顽疾。现有方法要么分别处理这两种行为，要么在每轮对话中广泛干预，导致本已正确的回答被不必要地劣化。因此需要一个统一框架来同时且精准地控制这两种行为。

Method: 采用推理时间干预（ITI）方法：从对比临床对中学习幻觉和谄媚各自的独立转向方向，并将其施加到经过因果验证的注意力头上。在推理时，通过行为特定门控判断何时需要干预：幻觉组件抑制无根据的声明，谄媚组件缓解因用户压力导致的答案偏移。模型权重保持冻结。

Result: 在基于EHR数据的临床问题上进行了15,900次模型响应评估。对于40亿参数模型，在600条压力轨迹中，未施转向的模型在570例中屈服；而门控转向帮助模型在551例中坚持更久。其在压力下的坚持水平与超过1000亿参数的模型相当。

Conclusion: 有靶向的推理时间转向可以在不干预每一轮对话的情况下显著提升模型对临床问答的鲁棒性，同时解决幻觉和谄媚两个问题，且效果可与超大模型媲美。

Abstract: Sycophancy and hallucination are persistent failure modes of Large Language Models (LLMs) across domains. However, it becomes particularly consequential in clinical question answering, where responses must remain grounded in the provided context and robust to user pressure. Hallucination can introduce information that is unsupported by the context, while sycophancy can cause a model to abandon a previously correct answer when challenged by the user. Existing approaches, such as prompt-based safeguards and always-on activation steering, often address these behaviors separately or apply interventions broadly across turns, which can unnecessarily deteriorate responses that were already correct. To address these limitations within a single framework, we employ Inference Time Intervention (ITI) to jointly control both behaviors by learning separate steering directions for hallucination and sycophancy from contrastive clinical pairs and applying them to causally verified attention heads. During runtime, behavior-specific gates then determine when intervention is needed: the hallucination component mitigates unsupported claims, while the sycophancy component mitigates answer shifts caused by user pressure. We evaluate this framework on clinical questions grounded in EHR data while keeping the model weights frozen. Across all evaluation settings, we conducted 15,900 model-response runs. Across 600 pressure trajectories for the 4-billion-parameter model, the unsteered model caved in 570 cases. At the same time, gated steering helped it last longer in 551 of them. It held its ground under pressure at levels comparable to those of models with more than 100 billion parameters, showing that targeted inference-time steering can improve robustness without intervening at every turn.

</details>


### [115] [Automata from Agent Traces: Failure and Next-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23670)
*Seonglae Cho,Franklin Cardenoso Fernandez,Umar Mohammed,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的多步行为轨迹语料库整体压缩为单个紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），作为行为结构基底，用于下一状态预测和失败预测。在12个公开数据集上，FSM紧凑（7-43个状态）、高保真（>=0.997拟合度）、构建快速（毫秒级），下一状态预测优于Agent Workflow Memory，失败预测AUROC最高达0.94，并能从部分轨迹实现早期停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行多步任务时行为结构不透明，长轨迹难以进行安全审计和运行时监控。现有方法仅按轨迹或仅对成功轨迹进行分析，忽略了跨运行（cross-run）的拓扑结构，无法同时服务下一步预测和失败预测两个目标。

Method: 将整个轨迹语料库坍缩为单个紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），作为LLM智能体不可预测行为的结构基底。该FSM作为共享结构底质，支持两种预测任务：基于FSM状态的下一状态预测，以及基于逐状态行为特征的失败预测（含在线监控和早期停止）。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上，FSM仅含7-43个状态，对保留数据重放拟合度>=0.997，跨数据划分拓扑近乎一致，构建时间在毫秒级。下一状态预测在全部真实标注匹配的数据集上优于Agent Workflow Memory。失败预测的保留AUROC最高达0.94，在线监控能从部分轨迹区分失败与成功运行并提前触发早停。

Conclusion: 行为拓扑主要由部署环境（harness）塑造而非LLM本身，FSM为安全审计和运行时监控提供了模型无关的结构性原语，是实用的通用方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-step tasks, but their behavioral structure remains opaque: long unstructured traces resist the safety auditing and runtime monitoring that deployment requires. Existing approaches operate per-trace or success-only, so they miss the cross-run topology that links next-step and failure prediction. To recover that shared structure, we collapse an entire trace corpus into a single, compact finite-state machine (FSM) that serves as a structural substrate for the otherwise unpredictable behavior of LLM agents. Across twelve public datasets, the FSMs are compact (7-43 states), replay held-out data at >=0.997 fitness with near-identical topology across splits, and build in milliseconds. This substrate addresses both prediction goals. For next-step prediction, FSM-state context outperforms Agent Workflow Memory on every ground-truth-matched dataset. For failure prediction, per-state behavioral features reach held-out AUROC up to 0.94, and an online monitor ranks failing runs above passing ones from a partial trace, triggering early stopping well before completion. Behavioral topology thus appears shaped more by the deployment harness than by the LLM, providing a model-agnostic structural primitive for safety auditing and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [116] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Station的开源多智能体环境，其中来自不同模型家族的AI智能体在没有中央协调者或脚本化流程的情况下自主协作从事数学研究。在AlphaEvolve目录中的12个构造问题及两个额外案例研究中，Station在五个问题上取得了相对于先前文献的新结果，包括有限域Kakeya集的无限族、11维604点亲吻构型、离散Kakeya针问题与符号不确定性问题的新纪录，以及Erdős最小重叠问题的改进下界，且智能体不仅生成了数值构造，还提供了定理和分析解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI系统能否在没有人类直接指导或预设流程的情况下，自主进行开放式的数学发现，并且生成不仅可验证而且可解释的数学结果。

Method: 构建Station环境，这是一个开放世界多智能体系统，不同模型家族的AI智能体自主选择研究方向、设计实验、相互协作并建立共享的科学文献库，通过无中心协调的方式针对目标问题进行探索。

Result: 在12个AlphaEvolve构造问题及2个额外案例中，Station在5个问题上取得了相对于现有文献的新成果：发现有限域Kakeya集的无限新族、维度11中精确604点亲吻构型、离散化Kakeya针问题与符号不确定性问题的新纪录，以及Erdős最小重叠问题的显著改进下界；此外还发现了Book Ramsey数的新无限族。

Conclusion: 自主多智能体系统能够在数学领域实现真正的开放式发现，且能产出具有理论解释的成果而非仅数值构造，表明AI辅助数学研究在无脚本化约束的环境下具有可行性和潜力。

Abstract: We study autonomous mathematical discovery in the Station, an open-world multi-agent environment in which AI agents from different model families pursue a shared research goal without a central coordinator or scripted pipeline. Agents choose their own research directions, conduct experiments, collaborate, and build a shared scientific literature. Across 12 construction problems from the AlphaEvolve catalogue and two additional case studies, the Station obtained results novel relative to the prior literature on five problems: a new infinite family of finite-field Kakeya sets, new exact 604-point kissing configurations in dimension 11, new records for the discretized Kakeya needle and sign uncertainty problems, and a substantially improved lower bound for Erdős's minimum-overlap problem. Agents also discovered novel infinite families for Book Ramsey numbers. Importantly, the agents produced not only numerical constructions but also theorems and analyses explaining how those constructions work, making the results more interpretable and easier for mathematicians to build upon. We release all raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification code, providing a transparent record of how these discoveries emerged.

</details>


### [117] [Do LLMs Understand Limit Order Book Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23706)
*Junxiao Chen,Paul Glasserman*
